### [1] [Agentic AI Containment Architecture for Security Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26108)
*Mohamed ElBendary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将安全作为架构约束引入多智能体系统，定义六项约束并实现Propose-Verify-Act-Verify执行模型，以提高可验证性、可追溯性并降低若干关键威胁，同时明确剩余语义风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在自治协调、工具使用与持续学习场景中带来新的安全与治理风险，而现有的遏制（containment）手段通常是设计或部署后的附加层，缺乏体系化的结构性保障。作者希望把安全内建为架构属性，从设计层面限定可行行为并提高可验证性。

Method: 提出一种Agent Containment Architecture，将常规系统分析工件映射为在约束系统下的机器可验证合约；定义并组合六个相互作用的约束（职责分离、部署前一致性检查、价值流绑定、时间隔离、严格知识验证、确定性结构与过程完整性验证），以保证所有操作被契约化、独立验证并可追溯。

Result: 给出命题与正确性推理，将六个约束与对应威胁类的防御机制关联；通过简历筛选案例演示在对抗条件下亦能生成可审计且符政策的结果；明确区分结构完整性与语义安全，量化并显式化剩余语义风险。

Conclusion: 本论文的结论是：通过将安全视为体系结构属性并以一组显式约束限定多智能体系统的设计空间，可以实现可机验、可追溯且有理据的“Propose-Verify-Act-Verify”执行模型，从而在结构层面显著降低若干关键威胁（如提示注入、协同操纵、跨会话中毒等）的可行性，并使剩余的语义风险可度量和可管理。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems are increasingly deployed in contexts where autonomous coordination, tool use, and continuous learning introduce novel security and governance risks. The containment approach to multi-agent system security remains underdeveloped, primarily resorting to add-on layers post-design and sometimes post-implementation and deployment. This paper proposes an Agent Containment Architecture that treats security as an architectural property enforced through a set of explicit constraints that bound the design space of multi-agent systems. The architecture proposed in this paper introduces a novel formal organizational mapping from standard systems analysis artifacts to machine-verifiable contracts under a proposed constraint system. The architecture introduces six interacting constraints: separation of responsibility assignments, pre-deployment coherence checking, value stream binding, temporal isolation, strict knowledge verification before accumulation, and deterministic verification of structural and process integrity. Together, these constraints enforce a Propose-Verify-Act-Verify execution model in which all operations are contractually defined, independently verified, and traceable to specific execution contexts. The paper presents propositions and correctness reasoning arguments linking these constraints to defenses against key threat classes, including prompt injection, orchestrator manipulation, cross-session state poisoning, and emergent agent collusion. A resume screening case study demonstrates how the architecture produces auditable, policy-compliant outcomes under adversarial conditions. The work explicitly separates structural integrity from semantic safety, bounding residual risks while making residual semantic risk explicit and measurable.

</details>


### [2] [Four Ways to Forge a Bundle My Own Verifier Calls Clean: Refusal-Site Mutation Testing of an Evidence-Bundle Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26183)
*Erik Hill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Paper exposes widespread vacuous-pass failures in a verifier, introduces an extraction rule and measurement protocol to enumerate and score refusal sites, shows simple real forgeries, and reports iterative fixes—while relying mainly on self-measurement in a closed-loop repository.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ensure that a third party can re-run claims offline and get the same answers; detect and quantify cases where verifiers claim to refuse but actually accept (vacuous passes); move from anecdotes to systematic measurement.

Method: Constructed a verifier protocol, obtained an external audit that revealed a forgery, merged audit fixes, then applied author-built instrumentation and an extraction rule (Section 6) to locate "refusal sites"; evaluated a fixture corpus and iteratively tested/fixed defects, reporting quantitative scores across commits/versions.

Result: Found multiple trivial forgeries (as small as four bytes or one capitalization change); initially identified 112 refusal sites with 75 deletable without breaking tests (score 0.330); targeted testing raised detection to 0.941 then 1.000 over different denominators; several measuring tools themselves exhibited vacuous success; the study is predominantly self-measured with one external audit as validation.

Conclusion: The paper documents that practical verifiers commonly exhibit “vacuous pass” defects (checks that report success without examining inputs), demonstrates simple forgeries that bypass claims, and proposes/uses a measurement methodology to extract and quantify verifier refusal sites; results are largely self-measured in a closed-loop registry with one external audit.

Abstract: I built a protocol whose premise is that a stranger can re-run my claims offline and get the same answer. An outside engineer audited it and broke it: a bundle whose headline numbers were false verified clean, the cheapest forgery four bytes. I merged his fix, then pointed my own instruments at the fixed verifier and found the same defect four more times, in places his audit did not reach. The cheapest is one capital letter.
  The unifying defect is not cryptographic or exotic: a check that reports success along a path where it never examined anything. Vacuous pass is a working label, not a discovery; Section 4 names the literatures already occupying it.
  So I stopped collecting anecdotes and measured. At f59fb62, under the extraction rule of Section 6, the verifier exposes 112 refusal sites; 75 could be deleted with the whole suite and every tamper fixture still green, a score of 0.330. Three of the four hand-found forgeries fall in surviving classes; the fourth is an obligation with no refusal site. Scored alone, the sixteen-fixture corpus built to prove the verifier can refuse catches 10. Testing the refusals themselves took it to 0.941, then to 1.000 at 92e4548 over a grown population of 146 sites; those denominators differ and the series between them is non-monotone, so Section 7.5 carries all eleven, not just the five rows of Table 2. Fixing the four found defects instead moved 37/112 to 39/119, leaving the pre-existing sites at 37.
  Seven times during this study my own measuring tools reported success while measuring nothing; four were built to detect this class, and one returned a perfect 1.000.
  Every number here is self-measured on a system I wrote, over a registry that is a closed loop of my own repositories; the one external data point is the audit of Section 2.2. That is stated here rather than buried: it is the paper's credibility, not a caveat.

</details>


### [3] [Cost-Utility Alignment in LLM Agent Trajectories:Profiling,Attribution,Diagnosis,Adaptation,and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26195)
*Dan Liu,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个轨迹级的成本-效用对齐框架，以五阶段分析流程和按证据强度排序的效用归因方法为核心，识别并修正代理执行中的五类错配，从而将资源消耗与任务贡献建立闭环对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理在多步轨迹执行中积累了代币、延迟、费用和环境风险等成本，但其带来的效用通常仅在任务级别体现。现有工作各自聚焦于推理优化、能力或评估，缺乏将成本与任务贡献进行轨迹级对齐的工具。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个能基于因果证据判定轨迹资源支出是否值得的分析框架。

Method: 构建理论框架并将其组织为五个分析阶段；提出并分类效用归因方法以提供因果证据；应用框架评估现有代理系统与方法，识别常见错配类型并推荐针对性适配措施；讨论评估协议覆盖效率、可靠性与经济价值，最后形成闭环的资源-效用分析流程。

Result: 提出并详细阐述了轨迹中心的成本-效用对齐框架，强调效用归因的重要性并按证据强度分类归因方法；通过实例分析了现有代理系统与评估协议，识别了五类常见错配并给出针对性适配建议；最终形成一个将成本侧与效用侧连接的闭环分析流程，为资源感知的代理设计与部署提供结构化依据。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一个以轨迹为中心的成本-效用对齐框架，将资源消耗与任务贡献作为双重账本对同一执行进行分析，组织为五个阶段：成本剖析、效用归因、错配诊断、针对性适配与评估。核心在于效用归因，按证据强度从过程代理、信息依赖到反事实重放进行分类，提供因果证据以支撑诊断与适配。通过该框架，作者分析了近期代理系统、归因方法与评估协议，并归纳出五类错配及其对应适配策略，形成连接代理执行成本与效用的闭环分析流程。

Abstract: LLM agents execute tasks through multi-step trajectories that accumulate cost in tokens, latency, monetary fees, and environmental risk while producing utility only at the aggregate task level. Prior surveys address inference optimization, agent capabilities, or evaluation in isolation, leaving practitioners without principled tools to determine whether a trajectory's resource expenditure is justified by its task contribution. We address this gap by developing a trajectory-centric cost-utility alignment framework that treats resource consumption and task contribution as dual ledgers over the same execution, organized around five analytical stages: cost profiling, utility attribution, misalignment diagnosis, targeted adaptation, and evaluation. Utility attribution is central to this structure: rather than relying on aggregate outcomes, it organizes contribution methods by evidential strength, from process proxies and information dependency to counterfactual replay, supplying the causal evidence that grounds diagnosis and guides adaptation. Using this framework, we analyze recent agent systems, attribution methods, and evaluation protocols covering efficiency, reliability, and economic value, as well as five forms of misalignment spanning cognitive and context use, external interaction, recovery-loop control, resource-capability allocation, and multi-agent coordination, together with their targeted adaptations. The result is a closed analytical loop connecting the cost side of agent execution to its utility side, providing a structured basis for resource-aware agent design and deployment.

</details>


### [4] [Harness Engineering for Predictable Agentic Systems: An Empirical Study of Deterministic Execution Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26197)
*Saransh Dhage*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 结构化的、在调用工具前校验计划的 harness 工程能显著消除 agent 的运行随机性并提升成功率，但性能与延迟影响依赖模型，应作具体测量和权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 在相同任务与工具下存在显著运行间差异，虽然可接受于探索性用途，但在金融、合规等受监管场景中不可接受；目标是通过工程手段提升可重复性与可靠性。

Method: 将 agent 包裹在确定性执行层中，包含有限状态控制、强制工具选择、输出校验、有界重试、结构化规划；对两类合成任务（金融、法律）和两款开源模型（Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct、Gemma-3-27B）进行 N=100 的实验，衡量 Reproducibility Rate、Determinism Index、任务成功率、token 成本和延迟。跟踪 trace 发现：当工具/状态/输出序列一致后，自由文本规划成主导方差来源；引入事先基于固定 schema 的 Structured Planning 后可消除该方差。

Result: 初始 harness 在 4 个模型-任务组合中效果参差——1 个显著提升、2 个显著退化、1 个无影响；加入 Structured Planning 后，3/4 组合在 N=100 时达到 Reproducibility Rate 与 Determinism Index = 1.000，并且 3/4 组合任务成功率升至 100%；token 成本在所有组合下降，但延迟在模型间出现显著分歧（一个变快，一个变慢）。

Conclusion: Harness 工程（在 agent 外层加确定性执行层并引入 Structured Planning）是提高 LLM 基础的 agent 在受监管环境中可重复性与任务成功率的有效方法，但其收益与成本高度依赖于所用模型，且需要具体测量而非假设。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) based agents exhibit substantial run-to-run execution variance even when given identical tasks and tools -- acceptable for exploratory use but unacceptable in regulated domains such as finance and compliance. We study harness engineering: wrapping an agent in a deterministic execution layer (finite-state control, forced tool selection, output validation, bounded retry, and structured planning) and measuring its effect on execution determinism and task success. Across two synthetic tasks (finance and legal) and two open-weight models (Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, Gemma-3-27B), a first-pass harness produces a mixed result: it significantly improves reproducibility in one of four model-task cells, significantly degrades it in two, and has no effect in the fourth. A trace-level diagnostic finds the cause: once tool sequence, state sequence, and output are already highly consistent, an unconstrained free-text planning step becomes the dominant remaining source of variance. Adding Structured Planning -- validating the plan against a fixed schema before any tool is invoked -- eliminates the effect entirely: three of four cells reach a Reproducibility Rate and Determinism Index of 1.000 at N=100, and task success rises to 100% in three of four cells. The gain is not free: token cost falls in every cell, but latency shows a genuine, sample-size-robust split by model -- one becomes faster under the constraint, the other markedly slower. We argue harness engineering is a distinct, effective discipline for agent reliability, but its cost is model-dependent and must be measured, not assumed.

</details>


### [5] [Fairness Invariants: A Relational Approach to Explaining and Mitigating Fairness Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26209)
*Ranit Debnath Akash,Ashish Kumar,Gang Tan,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REMI把反事实公平性当作关系不变量发现，通过在成对样本上学习双向规则不变量来解释、定位并缓解个体歧视，能在不重训练模型的前提下显著降低不公平决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动系统在高风险社会经济领域存在个体歧视，但现有解释方法偏向单输入决策，难以描述本质为比较性的反事实差异；缺乏可定位、可解释并可直接缓解的机制。

Method: 受到循环不变量合成的启发，构建双向关系解释框架：在成对原始/反事实样本上强制双向相等约束，结合三种数据对齐技术学习可解释规则模型（即公平不变量）；这些规则用于判定输入空间中不满足公平性的区域并采取阻断/重标记策略。

Result: 在符号程序与神经网络上评估，REMI在定位真实公平缺陷上成功率>83%，显著优于现有基线，并在黑箱模型中将歧视性决策减少至多70%。

Conclusion: REMI将个体公平性解释与定位问题形式化为关系不变量发现问题，通过在成对样本(x,x')上学习双向约束，推导出可解释的基于规则的不变量，作为“公平护栏”来局部阻断或重标记不公平预测，从而无需模型重训练即可缓解个体歧视。

Abstract: Data-driven software systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes socio-economic domains, from criminal justice to financial lending. However, these systems often exhibit individual discrimination---unjustified disparities in which a program yields different outcomes for similar individuals who differ only in their protected attributes (e.g., race, gender, age). While existing research has focused on detecting and quantifying these bugs, there remains a critical lack of principled mechanisms to explain and localize individual fairness bugs. Current explanation techniques are largely designed for single-input decisions rather than the relational nature of discrimination, which inherently involves a comparison between an original and a counterfactual pair.
  We present REMI, a framework for the automated localization, explanation, and mitigation of individual discrimination. Inspired by loop-invariant synthesis in formal methods, we treat counterfactual fairness as a relational invariant discovery problem. We introduce a bidirectional relational explanation framework that learns over paired examples $(x, x')$ to identify regions of the input space where fairness is violated. Unlike traditional one-way implication pairs used in invariant inference, our approach enforces bidirectional constraints: requiring identical outcomes for both original and counterfactual samples. REMI utilizes three data-alignment techniques to infer interpretable rule-based models that act as "fairness invariants." These rules serve as guardrails to selectively block or relabel unfair predictions without requiring model retraining. Our evaluation on symbolic and neural network programs demonstrates that REMI localizes ground-truth fairness bugs in over 83% of cases, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and reducing discriminatory decisions in black-box models by up to 70%.

</details>


### [6] [Characterizing the Landscape of Open-Source Satellite Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26211)
*Jinfeng Wen,Qi Liang,Yuehan Sun,Federica Sarro,Ao Zhou,Xuanzhe Liu,Shangguang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过对22,286个GitHub卫星项目的挖掘和646个项目的人工分类，首次刻画了开源卫星软件生态：快速增长、目标多样、实现异质，并提出实践性建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着航天应用增多和卫星软件复杂性上升，社区对开源卫星软件生态的系统性、经验性理解不足，需要一项全面的实证研究来揭示现状并指导实践。

Method: 基于GitHub挖掘与混合方法分析：检索并收集22,286个与卫星相关的GitHub项目；围绕三个研究问题（流行度趋势、软件目标、开发实践）进行量化分析；对646个项目做人工检查以构建包含43类的软件目标分类法；对含源代码的项目做深入静态/元数据分析以统计语言、实现策略等。

Result: （1）项目与活跃开发者数量随时间上升，受关注度增长；（2）提取出43类软件目标，覆盖从概念设计、数据集、系统实现到仿真与测试等多个层面；（3）源码分析显示生态高度异质、任务专用化，涉及66种编程语言与多样实现策略；（4）总结关键发现并导出若干对开发者与研究者有指导意义的建议。

Conclusion: 本文是首个针对开源卫星软件的系统特征研究，发现该生态正快速增长且高度异质化，软件目标涵盖43类，使用66种编程语言，呈现出任务专用化与实现多样化的特点，并给出面向开发者和研究者的可操作性建议。

Abstract: Satellites have become fundamental components of modern technological systems, supporting critical infrastructure in communication, navigation, Earth observation, and scientific research. As space exploration advances and demand for satellite-enabled services grows, reliance on complex, heterogeneous satellite software continues to increase. A systematic understanding of the satellite software landscape is therefore increasingly important, yet existing studies still lack a comprehensive empirical examination. To address this gap, we present the first characterization study of open-source satellite software, examining its ecosystem and development practices. We mine and analyze 22,286 satellite-related GitHub projects through three research questions on popularity trends (RQ1), software goals (RQ2), and development practices (RQ3). First, we characterize the temporal evolution of projects and active developers, revealing increasing popularity. Second, through manual inspection of 646 projects, we construct a taxonomy of 43 software-goal categories spanning conceptual design, datasets, system implementation, simulation, testing, and tools. Third, we conduct an in-depth analysis of projects with source code, revealing a highly heterogeneous and task-specialized ecosystem with 66 programming languages and diverse implementation strategies. Finally, we summarize key findings and derive actionable implications for satellite developers and researchers.

</details>


### [7] [Challenges and Contributions in Quality of AI-Based Software: A Systematic Mapping Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26215)
*Maryum Hamdani,Mateen Ahmed Abbasi,Marko Jäntti,Markku Tukiainen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项涵盖2020–2026年文献的系统映射研究（33篇）总结出AI软件质量领域的六大挑战，强调当前评估模型与度量不足，并呼吁学术—产业—标准化组织协作开发可操作的质量评估框架与测量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI深度嵌入现代软件系统，传统的软件质量概念与评估方法面临挑战，需要系统归纳当前研究现状、识别关键问题并指引后续研究与工业实践。

Method: 采用系统映射研究（Systematic Mapping Study），检索时间范围为2020年1月到2026年1月，从五个电子数据库进行自动化检索、筛选，并辅以雪橇式追溯，共纳入33项一手研究。

Result: 识别出六类反复出现的挑战（其中最突出的是现有质量评估模型的局限），其次为非功能需求管理、质量感知的开发流程、质量保证活动、度量与工具不足以及标准化缺失。研究建议跨界协作以制定综合质量评估及其度量方法。

Conclusion: 系统映射研究表明，目前有关AI驱动软件质量的研究分布分散、证据有限，主要挑战包括现有质量评估模型不足、非功能需求管理欠缺、质量感知开发与质量保障实践薄弱等。研究呼吁学术界、工业界与标准化组织协同，制定全面的质量评估框架及度量方法。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in modern software systems, raising important questions about how its quality should be defined, assessed, and assured. This paper presents a Systematic Mapping Study (SMS) on the quality of AI-based software. The study synthesizes primary studies published between January 2020 and January 2026 and selected from five electronic data sources. A total of 33 primary studies were included after automated search, screening, and snowballing. The results identify six recurring challenge categories, with the most prominent being limitations in existing quality assessment models, followed by issues in non-functional requirement management, quality-aware development, and quality assurance. The findings suggest a call for collaboration of researchers and industrial practitioners with standardization organizations, that could possibly devise comprehensive quality assessments and their measurement methods.

</details>


### [8] [The Green Software Landscape: A Systematic Mapping Study on Evolution, Applications, Software Lifecycle, and Best Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26229)
*Max Hort,Maria Kechagia,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统映射显示Green SE研究在2023年后快速增长（AI驱动），以优化与基准测试为主，但能耗实验在硬件与测量报告、稳定性与可复现性方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To provide the SE community with a consolidated view of research trends, experimental practices, and gaps in energy-focused software engineering, thereby guiding future sustainable software efforts.

Method: Systematic mapping study of literature from 2010–2024, collecting 390 publications classified by application domain and research type; deeper analysis on a representative subset of 79 papers focusing on experimental elements (hardware, measurement, stability, replicability).

Result: Most energy-related works appear in SE conferences; clear rise in publications since 2023 led by AI topics; optimization and benchmarking are the most common study types; experimental reporting often misses key details affecting reproducibility.

Conclusion: The field of Green Software Engineering has grown, with a notable surge around 2023 driven by AI-related work; optimization and benchmarking dominate, and many experimental studies lack full transparency and replicability details.

Abstract: Energy consumption and climate change have made sustainability critical in Software Engineering (SE), driving the emergence of Green SE. Over the past 15 years, numerous solutions for sustainable software systems have been published by the SE community, offering a rich resource for analyzing the field's evolution. To explore this, we conducted a systematic mapping study of Green SE research published between 2010 and 2024. We collected 390 publications, categorizing them by application domain (e.g., mobile, cloud, AI) and research type (e.g., optimisation study, benchmarking, literature review Additionally, we analyzed a representative subset of 79 papers to classify the key elements-such as hardware, measurement, stability, and replicability-considered during energy measurement experiments. Our findings indicate that SE conferences host the majority of energy-related literature. Notably, Green SE studies surged in popularity starting in 2023, largely driven by AI-related publications. Optimization and benchmarking emerged as the most prevalent research types. Ultimately, we aim to inform the SE community about current approaches to energy concerns, highlight critical experimental practices, and advocate for continued action toward more sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [9] ["A Second Set of Eyes": The Process and Challenges of Software Documentation Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26232)
*Avinash Bhat,Ian Arawjo,Disha Shrivastava,Jin L. C. Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对31位技术写作者的访谈表明，文档质量依赖五个互补的审查阶段与跨职能协作，但受限于组织协调与工具支持不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档写作所需知识分散在开发者、经理等多方，既有质量标准但缺乏对从业者如何协作提升文档质量及其所遇挑战的研究，研究旨在填补该空白并为流程与工具改进提供依据。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈，样本为来自不同组织的31名有经验技术写作者，对其日常文档审查实践、角色分工、面临的挑战及所使用的工具进行质性分析。

Result: 研究通过对31名经验丰富的技术写作者进行半结构化访谈，揭示了文档审查所需的个人与协作努力，识别出自审、技术审查、编辑审查、试玩（play testing）和发布后反馈五个阶段，每个阶段依赖具有不同专长的从业者来解决内容、呈现和用户体验层面的质量问题；同时指出了组织与技术层面的挑战，如召集专家审稿、协调开发时间表以及文档工作流缺乏针对性工具。

Conclusion: 文档审查是一个多阶段、跨职能的协作过程，当前组织与工具支持不足，需在流程与工具层面进行改进以提升文档质量。

Abstract: Organizations assign documentation work to technical writers, yet the knowledge required to produce it is distributed across developers, managers, and other practitioners. Prior work has established quality criteria for judging "good" documentation, but it has not examined how practitioners bring that expertise to improve documentation quality or the challenges they face in doing so. Through semi-structured interviews with experienced technical writers ($n=31$) from different organizations, our work reveals the individual and collaborative effort required to maintain documentation quality. We identify five distinct stages of the documentation review process: self review, technical review, editorial review, play testing, and post-publication feedback. Each stage draws on practitioners with distinct expertise to address quality across content, presentation, and user experience. Our findings surface organizational and technical challenges writers face in recruiting expert reviewers, navigating development timelines, and contending with tools not specifically designed for documentation workflows. Our work positions documentation review as a crucial yet understudied site of collaborative work and opens new research and design directions for process improvement and tool support.

</details>


### [10] [When Review Alone No Longer Scales: Layered Supervision in AI-Assisted Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26316)
*Markus Stolze,Mirco Strässle*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: High-throughput AI code generation overloads traditional review processes. Teams respond by layering guardrails—preventive, executable, and supervisory human oversight—so supervision responsibilities are distributed rather than borne by code review alone.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-assisted development dramatically increases the speed and volume of generated code, straining traditional quality-control mechanisms (code review, linting, testing, CI/CD) and raising questions about how guardrails evolve to manage high-throughput generation.

Method: Qualitative interview study with five software engineering practitioners, situated within a broader practitioner survey; likely thematic analysis of interview transcripts to identify patterns and categories.

Result: Findings show supervision work is distributed across multiple layers: preventive guardrails externalize intent and conventions, executable guardrails are repurposed as scalable supervision infrastructure, and human reviewers move from line-by-line inspection to supervisory interpretation focused on architecture and long-term concerns, describing a shift toward layered supervision.

Conclusion: Organizations are transitioning from review-centric guardrails to layered supervision: preventive guardrails that encode architecture and conventions in machine-interpretable form; executable guardrails that scale supervision via linting, testing, and CI/CD; and human oversight that shifts to higher-level architectural reasoning, explainability, and maintainability. No single guardrail is sufficient alone.

Abstract: AI-assisted development tools enable software engineers to generate implementations at substantially higher speed and volume than in traditional workflows. Software teams have long relied on guardrails -- standing control mechanisms such as code review, linting, testing, and CI/CD pipelines -- to maintain quality and coordination. High-throughput AI-assisted generation increases pressure on these guardrails -- straining their capacity to keep pace with the volume and rate of generated changes -- and reshapes how organizations supervise development workflows, yet relatively little is known about how existing guardrails evolve in response. We conducted a qualitative interview study with five software engineering practitioners, situated within a broader practitioner survey. Our findings indicate that organizations distribute the work of supervision across multiple guardrail layers: preventive guardrails (produced by externalizing architectural intent and conventions into machine-interpretable form), executable guardrails (linting, testing, and CI/CD repurposed as scalable supervision infrastructure), and human oversight (shifting from line-by-line inspection toward supervisory interpretation focused on architectural reasoning, explainability, and long-term maintainability). We characterize this as a transition from review- centric guardrails toward layered supervision, in which no single guardrail carries the supervision load alone.

</details>


### [11] [Investigating Software Aging in LLM-Generated Software Systems across Generation-and-Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26391)
*Cesar Santos,Michele Vitagliano,Roberto Natella,Ermeson Andrade*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM-generated backend services often develop memory-usage-based aging during 48h continuous runs, so functional correctness alone is insufficient—operational testing and mitigation are necessary before deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate long-term operational reliability (software aging) of LLM-generated services under sustained execution, beyond prior work focused on functional correctness, security, or robustness.

Method: Generated service-based applications for JavaScript, Python, and Rust using LLM-based generation platforms from BaxBench backend scenarios; validated functionality with BaxBench-derived tests; executed 48-hour workload runs while monitoring memory usage, response time, and throughput; applied Mann--Kendall trend test and Sen's slope estimator for time-series analysis; complemented runtime measurements with static source-code analysis and an exploratory comparison to human-written implementations.

Result: Memory usage exhibited the most consistent statistically significant upward trends across most application-language combinations, indicating potential aging; response time and throughput showed heterogeneous behavior; static analysis uncovered plausible code-level aging mechanisms; human-written systems can also manifest similar aging trends.

Conclusion: Operational reliability testing (runtime aging analysis, observability, static/dynamic analysis) must be integrated into the LLM-generation pipeline; adopt monitoring, profiling, resource limits, and engineering practices to detect and mitigate aging prior to production deployment.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate executable software systems from natural language specifications, accelerating development and reducing manual implementation effort. Although recent studies have investigated the functional correctness, security, maintainability, and robustness of LLM-generated code, little is known about the long-term reliability of such systems under sustained execution. In this paper, we experimentally investigate software aging symptoms in LLM-generated service-based applications across different programming languages. Using backend scenarios derived from BaxBench, we generated applications targeting JavaScript, Python, and Rust through LLM-based generation platforms, validated them with BaxBench-derived tests, and subjected them to 48-hour workload executions. We monitored memory usage, response time, and throughput and analyzed them using the Mann--Kendall test and Sen's slope estimator. We further complemented the runtime evaluation with static analysis of the generated source code and an exploratory comparison with human-written implementations of related backend scenarios. The results show that memory usage is the most consistent indicator of potential software aging, with statistically significant upward trends in most application-language combinations, while response time and throughput exhibit more heterogeneous behavior. Static analysis identified plausible code-level aging mechanisms, and the comparison with human-written systems showed that aging trends can also emerge in manually developed implementations. These findings indicate that functional correctness alone is insufficient to assess the operational reliability of LLM-generated software before deployment in continuously running environments.

</details>


### [12] [Spec2Vision: Contract-Guided Delivery of AI-Generated Computer Vision Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26400)
*Ghfran Jabour,Sergey Ivanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过保持任务契约在分阶段生成—检查—修复流程中的显式传递，Spec2Vision在满足下游评估器要求方面显著优于仅追求代码可运行性的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是揭示并量化一个常见问题：自动生成的CV代码即便能运行，往往未必满足下游评估器所要求的任务契约；因此需要一个可测量、可修复的流程来缩小“可运行性”与“交付符合性”之间的差距。

Method: 提出并实现了Spec2Vision框架：通过分阶段运行时（生成、筛选、测试、有限修复）来产生并评估以规格为导向的CV流水线可执行包。基准覆盖17个CV任务、10个可执行条件，对每个任务-条件组合重复5次（总计850次主要运行）。在实验中进行了多项消融实验（移除结构性修复、兼容性支架、生成器预检等），并与多种单代理可执行基线比较（基线对任务规格暴露程度逐步增大）。

Result: 在850次主要运行中，完整Spec2Vision达成81/85的评估器测试通过；移除结构修复降至55/85，移除兼容性支架降至58/85，移除生成器预检降至39/85。单代理可执行基线表现较弱：从轻量化任务绑定的17/85增加到直接暴露源码-规格时的35/85。轻量基线虽在85/85次运行中核心可运行，但仅达17/85评估器通过与6/85严格交付成功，说明“可运行”并不等同于“交付符合”。同时，作者提供了可审计的工件以支持运行包、模型可见输入与派生表的审计。

Conclusion: 主要结论是：在生成的计算机视觉（CV）管道中，保持“任务契约”（task contract）在分阶段生成、检查与有界修复过程中的显式传递，能显著提高通过下游评估器的成功率；仅保证代码可运行并不等于满足任务交付要求（delivery）。

Abstract: Generated computer-vision code can be runnable without satisfying the task contract enforced by a downstream evaluator. We study that gap with Spec2Vision, an experimental framework for producing and evaluating specification-grounded CV pipeline bundles through a staged runtime that keeps the task contract explicit across synthesis, screening, testing, and bounded repair. The benchmark evaluates 17 CV tasks, 10 executable conditions, and 5 repeats per task-condition cell, for 850 primary runs. In the primary 850-run evaluation, Spec2Vision reaches 81/85 evaluator-test passes; removing structural repair drops to 55/85, compatibility scaffolding to 58/85, and generator preflight to 39/85. The executable single-agent baselines expose progressively richer task specifications to the model, culminating in direct source-spec exposure, yet remain much weaker overall, from 17/85 for lightweight task grounding to 35/85 evaluator-test passes. The lightweight baseline nevertheless remains core-runnable in 85/85 runs but reaches only 17/85 evaluator-test passes and 6/85 strict-delivery successes, showing that runnability is not equivalent to delivery. Across this benchmark, the strongest evidence comes from keeping the task contract explicit across staged generation, checking, and repair. Artifacts are provided to support audit of run bundles, model-visible inputs, and derived tables.

</details>


### [13] [STILL: Recovering Lowered STL Semantics for LLM-assisted C++ Decompilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26408)
*Xiaohan Wang,Kevin Leach*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: STILL: a compact, structured semantic interface predicting STL container semantics from stripped CFGs; when used as hints for LLM refinement it substantially increases executable-recovery rates, though benefits vary with model backbone and require further generalization work.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional decompilation and symbol stripping remove/obscure source-level semantics (container types, library-call structure), causing LLM-assisted decompilation to underperform on stripped C++ using STL; recovering these semantics should improve readability and re-executability.

Method: Design a structured semantic interface (STILL) that takes stripped control-flow graphs as input, predicts function-level STL container semantics (e.g., container types, slices) and emits compact, LLM-readable hints; use these hints to guide LLM-based refinement (DeepSeek-chat) of decompiler output.

Result: On StlBench, STILL accurately predicts common container-level STL semantics, strongest on stable string and vector slices. On stripped HumanEval decompilation, using STILL hints with DeepSeek-chat reaches 28.4% executability vs 17.4% without hints and 8.9% for raw Ghidra output. Hint effectiveness depends on downstream LLM backbone; decompilation-specialized models need light adaptation to use the hints effectively.

Conclusion: STILL demonstrably improves LLM-assisted decompilation for stripped C++ that uses the STL by predicting function-level container semantics and providing compact hints, leading to higher executability of recovered code.

Abstract: LLM-assisted decompilation improves readability and re-executability, but still underperforms on stripped C++ functions that use the Standard Template Library (STL). Compilation, optimization, and symbol stripping remove or obscure source-level semantics such as container types and library-call structure, while traditional decompiler output often fails to recover them. We present STILL, a structured semantic interface that predicts function-level STL container semantics from stripped control-flow graphs and renders them as compact hints for LLM refinement. On StlBench, STILL predicts common container-level STL semantics, with the strongest cross-dataset results for stable string and vector slices. On stripped HumanEval decompilation, these hints enable DeepSeek-chat refinement to reach 28.4% executability, compared with 17.4% for no-hint refinement and 8.9% for raw Ghidra decompilation; hint utility is downstream-backbone-dependent, with decompilation-specialized models requiring lightweight adaptation to benefit from the same interface.

</details>


### [14] [DeepRepro: State-Aware Subplanning for Paper-to-Code Reproduction in Evolving Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26557)
*Hongru Song,Ruqing Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过在执行过程中基于仓库状态生成细粒度子规划并结合仓库感知的编排与进程接口，DeepRepro 提高了论文到代码重现的成功率并优于现有代码 agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的论文到代码生成通常采用静态的预先规划并逐文件生成，这在面对依赖变更、接口调整和运行反馈时易导致不一致或失败；因此需要一种在执行过程中动态调整规划以保持实现与实际运行状态一致的方案。

Method: DeepRepro 包含三个核心组件：1) 将仓库状态与运行时反馈编码为细粒度子规划（execution-state-aware subplanning）；2) 仓库感知的编排器（repository-aware orchestration），用于按依赖关系和接口变更调度生成与修改任务；3) 轻量级的进程感知接口（process-aware interface），用于透明监控和交互。

Result: 在 PaperBench Code-Dev 基准上，DeepRepro 在多个指标上持续优于若干强基线（包括科研与商用代码 agent），表明状态感知的子规划与仓库感知编排能显著提升重现效果。

Conclusion: DeepRepro 提出了一种基于执行状态感知子规划的论文到代码重现框架，通过将不断演化的仓库状态与运行时反馈动态转换为细粒度的实现子计划，保证规划与执行同步，从而提升长程流水线中的一致性与成功率。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic large language models (LLMs) have enabled increasingly autonomous software engineering workflows, yet automatic machine learning (ML) paper-to-code reproduction remains a challenging long-horizon problem. Unlike conventional code generation, this task requires constructing and maintaining a fully functional repository whose state continuously evolves during execution. Existing systems typically rely on static upfront planning followed by sequential file-level generation, which often leads to inconsistencies as dependencies, interfaces, and execution feedback change over time. We propose DeepRepro, a state-aware framework for paper-to-code reproduction based on execution-state-aware subplanning. DeepRepro dynamically transforms evolving repository states and runtime feedback into fine-grained implementation subplans, keeping planning aligned with execution throughout repository construction. The framework further incorporates repository-aware orchestration and a lightweight process-aware interface for transparent monitoring of long-horizon reproduction. Experiments on PaperBench Code-Dev show that DeepRepro consistently outperforms strong scientific and commercial code-agent baselines.

</details>


### [15] [The Thousand-Graph Hypothesis: A Testable Hypothesis of Task-Conditioned Relation Materialization in Repository-Level Code Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26602)
*Fei Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 用实体级别外部索引 + 推理时按任务物化关系的两层检索架构，能在不构建预置关系图的前提下，提高对大型软件仓库的任务成功率，双层索引效果最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型代码/软件仓库超出模型上下文限制；对仓库进行持续训练昂贵且易过时；单纯的本地检索易遗漏分散需求；显式关系图维护代价高。需要一种既能处理分散信息又能免除持续维护关系图的可伸缩检索接口。

Method: 不在模型内编码完整仓库知识，而是保持实体级别的外部索引；在推理时按任务需要动态物化实体间关系。索引设计为两层：第一层负责全局路由定位相关实体集合，第二层在局部实体集合上聚焦并构建细粒度关系。评测在DeepSeek-V4-Flash和SWE-bench Verified上进行，比较基线、单层和双层条件下的成功率。

Result: 在没有预先构建实体-关系边的条件下，基线（base）、一层和两层条件分别实现成功率92.1%、94.2%和95.6%，表明动态物化关系与两层索引带来稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出的实体仅外部接口和任务条件化的推理时关系物化能够在无需预建实体关系边情况下，有效检索并利用分散在大型软件仓库的知识，从而提高任务成功率。两层索引（全局路由 + 局部实体关注）在评估中优于一层或基线方案。

Abstract: Large software repositories are often beyond model context limits. Training repository knowledge into models is costly and quickly stale, while local retrieval can miss scattered requirements, and explicit relation graphs add ongoing maintenance burden. We propose an entity-only external interface with task-conditioned relation materialization during inference. A two-layer index separates global routing from local entity focus and is evaluated on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and SWE-bench Verified. The base, one-layer, and two-layer conditions achieve 92.1%, 94.2%, and 95.6% success, respectively, under zero pre-built entity-relation edges.

</details>


### [16] [Processing/p5 Defined through Practice and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26614)
*Kit Kuksenok,Lee Tusman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过在 JavaScript 与 Lua 上构建 Processing/p5 实现的比较案例研究，论文抽象出一套跨语言的设计要素，形成一个用于指导创意编码库实现与实践的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到不同语言生态下的 Processing/p5 草图仍表现出相似性，意在识别出构成 Processing/p5 体验的核心设计维度，从而指导新的库实现、教学与艺术实践。

Method: 通过在两种宿主语言（JavaScript 与 Lua）中实现 Processing/p5 的案例研究，进行比较分析并从实现决策中抽象出共同要素；并将该框架与其它探索性创意工具的决策进行对照讨论。

Result: 归纳出一份可操作的指导性要素清单，说明每个要素如何在不同实现中被具体化，并指出这些要素对学习、研究和艺术创作的机会与影响。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一组独立于宿主语言的“Processing/p5 指导性决策要素”，认为这些要素构成了Processing/p5作为创意编码体验的一致优先级与设计特征。

Abstract: Processing/p5 libraries across different programming languages enact consistent priorities for creative coding as a designed experience. While different programming language ecosystems, like Java and JavaScript, are each associated with their own affordances, community norms, and patterns of use, Processing/p5 sketches across these languages share similarities. Based on case studies of building an implementation of Processing/p5 in two host languages, JavaScript and Lua, we propose a list of software decision-making guiding aspects that constitute Processing/p5, regardless of host language. We discuss this framework in the context of decisions in other exploratory and creative tools that demonstrate how each of the guiding aspects can be operationalized differently than in the case studies. The proposed list highlights opportunities for learning, research, and artistic practice through creation of new Processing/p5 libraries for creative coding and algorithmic art.

</details>


### [17] [FaultLens: Learning Compact Behavioral Test Suites for Generated Operational Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26746)
*Zeming Liu,Hang Lyu,Jingtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FaultLens通过一次性探测缓存加上故障驱动与多样性混合排序，能在极小探测预算下提供高度可审计的缺陷覆盖与实用部署收益，而非形式化正确性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写示例易漏稀疏边界与交互类缺陷而穷尽回归代价高昂，因而需要一种既紧凑又能保留可审计证据的测试构造方法。

Method: 一次性执行丰富的探测域并缓存稀疏的故障—探测器击杀关系；基于早期生成版本学习探测器排序；采用交替混合策略：基于故障的贪心组件利用已知击杀结构，基于变异无关的多样性组件覆盖探测器家族、用例、模板与时间箱。

Result: 在多环境、多种子和大规模程序—探测对评估中，32探测器混合策略在后续两代中覆盖576/582（99.0%）可动态击杀的缺陷，仅占穷尽域的1.2–2.0%；当从训练中剔除整族故障时，多样性组件将场景族宏覆盖率从84.6%提升至94.9%；部署研究显示保守的准入规则把严重尾部回归从15/20组降为0/20。

Conclusion: FaultLens能以可审计的证据连接，学习出紧凑且优先级明确的行为测试套件，从而在保留高缺陷覆盖率的同时大幅降低执行探测开销。

Abstract: Generated operational programs are often validated with either a few hand-written examples or exhaustive regression suites. The former can miss sparse boundary and interaction faults, while the latter can be unnecessarily expensive. We introduce FaultLens, a method for learning compact behavioral test suites while preserving an auditable connection to executed evidence. It executes a rich probe domain once, stores the fault-probe kill relation as a sparse outcome cache, and learns probe orderings only from earlier program generations. A fault-driven greedy component exploits known kill structure, while a mutation-independent diversity component covers probe families, cases, templates, and temporal bins. Their alternating hybrid remains useful when a new program contains a fault mechanism absent from ordering construction.
  We evaluate twenty generated operational policies across four environments, ten execution seeds, 1,200 measured run summaries, 2,160 controlled program transformations, and 4,120,200 executed program-probe pairs. Of 1,960 intended faulty transformations, 1,779 alter a contract or output somewhere in the finite audit domain; 200 additional controls preserve behavior. A 32-probe hybrid learned on generations 1-3 covers 576/582 (99.0%) dynamically killable faults in generations 4-5 using 1.2-2.0% of the exhaustive domain. With an entire fault family withheld from training, diversity raises scenario-family macro coverage from 84.6% to 94.9%. In a downstream deployment study, a conservative admission rule reduces severe tail regressions from 15/20 program-environment groups to 0/20. FaultLens provides a prioritized evidence mechanism, not a proof of correctness, and makes its budget, evidence source, generalization split, and misses explicit.

</details>


### [18] [Beyond Execution: Auditing Experimental Fidelity in LLM-Driven Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26753)
*Lezhi Yu,Xiaogang Xu,Yuhua Zhou,Shuibing He,Aimin Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ABE-Ralph is a structured, reference-anchored auditor that detects methodological hallucinations in LLM-driven experiments, achieving high robust execution and revealing important scientific failure modes while providing a workflow for faithful reproduction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents doing scientific experiments often commit ‘methodological hallucinations’—silently changing data, budgets, or components—so automated agents must be audited against the original reference method to produce trustworthy scientific evidence.

Method: Design of a structured auditing framework that encodes claims, protocols, components, baselines, and metrics as experimental constraints; an 8-step guided workflow for implementation; and multi-level verification (quantitative, qualitative, code-level) applied across long-horizon reproduction runs.

Result: Across 30 long-horizon reproductions spanning 12 ML domains, ABE-Ralph attained a 93% robust execution rate and uncovered five scientific failure modes. On 23 NatureBench tasks, it matched or exceeded prior state-of-the-art on 5 tasks, demonstrating practical utility but modest SOTA gains.

Conclusion: ABE-Ralph enables automated, reference-anchored auditing of LLM-driven experimental implementations, exposing methodological hallucinations and ensuring that experimental design and evidence align with paper claims. It achieves high robust execution and detects multiple scientific failure modes, arguing that faithful evaluation must check experimental fidelity, not just code execution.

Abstract: LLM agents used for scientific experimentation must do more than generate executable code: they must implement the reference method faithfully, design experiments that test the paper's claims, and provide evidence supporting those claims. We show that agents often produce methodological hallucinations: silently reducing datasets or training budgets, replacing failed learning or generative components with lookup or oracle functions, or drawing conclusions from resource-limited settings where a method's claimed advantage disappears. To detect these failures, we introduce ABE-Ralph, a reference-anchored auditing framework that represents claims, protocols, required components, baselines, and metrics as structured experimental constraints, guides implementation through an 8-step workflow, and performs quantitative, qualitative, and code-level verification. Across 30 long-horizon reproduction runs covering 12 machine learning domains, ABE-Ralph achieves a 93% robust execution rate and identifies five scientific failure modes. In 23 NatureBench discovery tasks, ABE-Ralph matches or exceeds state-of-the-art performance on 5 tasks. These results show that reliable evaluation of AI scientists must assess whether the experimental design faithfully tests the intended claim and whether the resulting evidence supports it, rather than treating code execution or plausible metrics as evidence of scientific success.

</details>


### [19] [An Empirical Evaluation of Using Large Language Models for Automated Model-Based Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27094)
*Hafize Sanli,Onur Kilincceker,Cihat Cetinkaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMs can shorten and optimize MBT test paths vs GraphWalker random baselines, but further rigorous, reproducible evaluation and hybrid/system-integration work are needed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address scalability and automation bottlenecks in industrial Model-Based Testing by evaluating whether LLMs can generate more efficient, shorter test sequences and thereby reduce manual effort and test execution cost.

Method: Empirical comparison of five state-of-the-art LLMs (GPT-5.1, GPT-5.2, Claude Opus 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Pro) against GraphWalker’s random and quick-random algorithms under edge and vertex coverage settings on four GraphWalker models (Parabank, Testinium, TLC, RISC-V) with escalating complexity; metrics include test-path length and step-size optimization.

Result: LLMs outperformed GraphWalker baselines in shortening test paths and reducing step sizes in the evaluated models, showing varying effectiveness by model complexity and LLM; results indicate promise but require more rigorous evaluation (statistical testing, cost/latency, validity checks).

Conclusion: LLMs demonstrate strong potential to produce shorter and more efficient model-based test paths than GraphWalker’s built-in random/quick-random algorithms across evaluated models, but further validation is needed for robustness, generalization, and industrial adoption.

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong potential for software engineering tasks, particularly software testing. Model-based testing (MBT) is a software testing technique. To address the broad scalability challenge for industrial adoption of MBTs, our paper presents an empirical evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) for automated model-based test generation, compared with a state-of-the-art model-based testing tool (GraphWalker) and its built-in algorithms (random and quick random for edge and vertex coverage settings). Our evaluation indicates strong potential to optimize and shorten test paths and step sizes using the recent five state-of-the-art LLMs (GPT-5.1, GPT-5.2, Claude Opus 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Gemini 2.5 Pro) against four GraphWalker models (two web applications (Parabank and Testinium) and two hardware applications (TLC and RISC-V) ) of escalating complexity.

</details>


### [20] [Mutation Testing for Reproducibility Safeguards in Machine Learning Research Software: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27100)
*Ilya Shulepov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 使用受控变异测试 39 个仓库后发现，现有验证工作流在样本中仅检测到很少的可复现性相关变更，表明应将变异测试作为补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习研究的可复现性受实验配置（如随机种子、依赖版本、数据分割、评估配置）影响，而现有仓库验证可能未对这些变化敏感，需一种方法评估验证机制能否约束关键实验选择。

Method: 开发并使用 MLReproMutate 对 39 个冻结的研究仓库案例施加四类受控变异（随机种子、依赖版本固定、数据划分、交叉验证折数），以盲结果方式评估这些变异是否被仓库内已有的验证工作流检测到；通过初始执行和有界恢复步骤扩大可评估样本。

Result: 在初始 39 个案例中，13 个产生直接可观测结果，通过有界恢复扩大到 24 个可评估案例（排除 1 个等价变异后为 23 个非等价变异）；被选验证工作流仅检测到其中 2 个，检测率为 8.7%。

Conclusion: 选定的验证工作流在样本中通常无法检测到受控的、与可复现性相关的变更，表明应将面向可复现性的变异测试作为对现有仓库验证的补充。

Abstract: Reproducibility in machine-learning research depends on experimental choices such as random seeds, dependency versions, data partitioning, and evaluation configuration. Existing repository validation workflows may execute successfully without detecting changes to such choices. We study this problem using MLReproMutate, research software that applies controlled, reproducibility-relevant mutations to ML research repositories and evaluates them against validation workflows already present in those repositories.
  We conducted an outcome-blind empirical study of 39 frozen repository-operator cases using four mutation classes: random seed, dependency pin, data split, and cross-validation fold count. Repository revisions, mutation candidates, and validation workflows were fixed before mutation outcomes were observed. Primary execution yielded outcomes for 13 of 39 cases; a bounded restoration procedure increased the combined evaluable set to 24. After excluding one confirmed-equivalent mutation, 23 confirmed non-equivalent mutations remained. The selected validation workflows detected 2 of these 23 mutations, corresponding to an observed detection proportion of 8.7%.
  These results do not imply that the corresponding repositories are irreproducible. Rather, they show that, in this sample, existing validation workflows often did not detect the particular controlled reproducibility-relevant changes introduced by the study. The findings motivate reproducibility-oriented mutation testing as a complementary way to assess whether research-software safeguards constrain experimentally important choices.

</details>


### [21] [AROMA+: A Study of Factors Affecting Reproducible Builds in the Maven Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27125)
*Mehdi Keshani,Amirhossein Rahmati,Mohammad Hossein Aref,Abbas Heydarnoori*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出并实现 AROMA+，自动从 Maven artifact 找到源码并恢复构建环境，实验证明对 Maven Central 有显著覆盖与高字段准确率，能扩展并校验手工维护的可重现性列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Java 供应链对 Maven 仓库高度依赖，但可重现构建在 Maven 生态中的研究与实践不足；现有的 Reproducible Central 列表手工维护成本高且不完整，因此探索自动化方法以扩展与验证该列表。

Method: 设计并实现工具 AROMA+，通过对制品及其元数据应用多重启发式方法（如仓库链接解析、构件元数据解析、源码仓库分析等）来定位源码并恢复发布时的环境信息，对 Maven Central 上的包进行批量尝试与再现。

Result: 与人工列表逐字段比对时最高可达99.8%准确率；发现自动化可为 Maven Central 上约32%的包提供可行的重现信息，其中12%包可完全重现；成功复现若干新包并贡献给 Reproducible Central，同时公开了数据集和工具。

Conclusion: 自动化从Maven发布件定位源码并恢复构建环境信息是可行的；AROMA+ 在字段级别与手工列表相比准确率高达99.8%，并能发现人工维护数据的缺陷。

Abstract: Modern software engineering establishes software supply chains and relies on tools and libraries to improve productivity. However, reusing external software in a project presents a security risk when the source of the component is unknown or the consistency of a component cannot be verified. Reproducible builds present a mitigation strategy, as they can confirm the origin and consistency of reused components. A large reproducibility community has formed for Debian, but the reproducibility of the Maven ecosystem, the backbone of the Java supply chain, remains understudied in comparison. Reproducible Central is an initiative that curates a list of reproducible Maven libraries, but the list is limited and challenging to maintain due to manual efforts. Our research aims to support these efforts in the Maven ecosystem through automation. We investigate the feasibility of automatically finding the source code of a library from its Maven release and recovering information about the original release environment. Our tool, AROMA+, can obtain this critical information from the artifact and the source repository through several heuristics and we use the results for reproduction attempts of packages on Maven Central. Overall, our approach achieves an accuracy of up to 99.8% when compared field-by-field to the existing manual approach. In some instances, we even detected flaws in the manually maintained list, such as broken repository links. We reveal that automatic reproducibility is feasible for 32% of the packages on Maven Central using AROMA+, and 12% of these packages are fully reproducible. We demonstrate our ability to successfully reproduce new packages and have contributed some of them to the Reproducible Central repository. Additionally, we highlight actionable insights, outline future work in this area, and make our dataset and tools publicly available.

</details>


### [22] [AgentDV: Closed-Loop Agentic AI for Hardware Design Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27148)
*Navya Goli,Junzhe Liu,Zhenge Jia,Umamaheswara Rao Tida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentDV converts single-shot LLM testbench generation into a tool-driven closed-loop pipeline that filters invalid outputs, anchors checks to CSRs, and iterates based on coverage, yielding much higher runnable and coverage-producing verification environments across multiple LLMs and benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL verification is time-consuming and error-prone; recent LLM-based automatic testbench generation often produces non-runnable, inconsistent, or low-coverage environments, motivating an automated closed-loop approach to make LLM outputs practically useful for SoC verification.

Method: Pipeline: (1) LLM analysis to produce testbench code and expected behaviors; (2) runnability filtering to reject syntactic/semantic invalid outputs via compilation/simulation checks; (3) CSR-grounded checking to align generated expected behavior with design control/status registers; (4) simulation and coverage measurement; (5) coverage-guided iteration to regenerate tests targeting uncovered features. Evaluated across three LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Llama, Qwen) on challenge DUTs and OpenTitan IPs with pass-rate and line/branch coverage metrics.

Result: AgentDV, a closed-loop agentic AI framework, combines LLM-guided analysis, testbench generation, simulation, coverage measurement, and iterative refinement with three key techniques—runnability filtering, CSR-grounded checking, and coverage-guided iteration—achieving high pass rates and coverage on evaluated DUTs (e.g., 100% pass on four DUTs and average 80.9% pass with Claude Sonnet 4.6) and substantially improving over single-shot prompting.

Conclusion: Closed-loop, tool-grounded verification significantly mitigates LLM hallucination and non-runnability in RTL testbench generation; AgentDV shows strong empirical gains but remains model- and benchmark-dependent, suggesting further work on generalization, deeper functional coverage, and reproducibility.

Abstract: Register-transfer level (RTL) verification consumes a major part of modern system-on-chip (SoC) development effort. Yet, recent LLM-based verification-code generation often fails to produce runnable, design-consistent, and coverage-producing testbenches. We present AgentDV, a closed-loop agentic AI framework for automated RTL verification environment generation. AgentDV transforms single-shot LLM testbench generation into a tool-grounded verification pipeline by combining LLM-guided analysis, testbench construction, simulation, coverage measurement, and iterative refinement. The framework introduces three key ideas: 1) runnability filtering to reject invalid generated environments, 2) CSR-grounded checking to reduce hallucinated signals and incorrect expected behavior, and 3) coverage-guided iteration to regenerate tests based on measured verification gaps. We evaluate AgentDV using three LLMs on challenge DUTs and public OpenTitan peripheral and security IP blocks. From our analysis, we observed that direct single-shot prompting fails to produce a valid coverage-producing environment on benchmarks. AgentDV achieves 100% pass rate on four DUTs and an average of 80.9% pass rate on all DUTs using Claude Sonnet 4.6. Similarly, an average of 58.7% and 60.6% pass rate is achieved for Llama and Qwen models, respectively. In addition, an average of 74.5%, 69.1%, and 64.9% of line coverage and 88.4%, 82.3%, and 76.7% of branch coverage for the benchmarks under consideration for Claude Sonnet 4.6, Llama, and Qwen models, respectively.

</details>


### [23] [A Trans-Domain Digital Twin for Bio-Aware Control of Climate and Energy in Cattle Fattening Barns Using Single-Episode Optimizer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27185)
*Mansoorali Amiri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一个把机理模型、增长模型、MPC与轻量级RL按5分钟/日两速率循环耦合的数字孪生控制框架，实现了气候—增长—能耗的联合控制原型，但仍需实地验证与调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在封闭育肥舍中，室内气候（温湿气流）与群体增重互为影响，影响舒适度、采食、代谢产热、日增重、饲料转化率与能耗；而体重变化反过来改变未来的热湿负荷与能耗需求。需要一个同时面向生物产出与能耗优化、能在运行中快速学习并融合不同领域模型的控制框架。

Method: 提出一个跨域数字孪生框架，具有单次学习能力，针对封闭牛育肥舍的生物感知气候与能量控制。该框架在多速率时间循环架构下集成：机理气候仿真器、家畜生长仿真器、模型预测控制（MPC）、轻量级强化学习（RL）和结构化知识记忆。快速环路每5分钟评估执行器决策以维持短期热舒适和能效；慢速环路基于日尺度气候、采食效率、产热和生长限制因素提供生物学指导。

Result: 证明了气候、增长、能耗、饲料、生物学指导与记忆可以在单个可执行控制周期内耦合；提出的多速率架构支持短期安全与长周期生物目标的协调。识别出的限制包括缺乏实地验证、饲料压力管理需改进、执行器命令突变需降低。

Conclusion: 框架在概念与仿真层面展示了跨域控制可行性与单次学习潜力，为生物感知暖通—能耗优化提供了系统路径；下一步应重点做参数辨识、鲁棒性分析、平滑执行器策略与小规模田间试验以验证实际效益与安全性。

Abstract: In closed cattle-fattening barns, the indoor climate and herd growth are mutually interdependent. Temperature, relative humidity, airflow, and ventilation affect thermal comfort, feed intake, metabolic heat production, daily growth, feed efficiency, and energy consumption, while body-weight gain alters the future heat and moisture loads of the barn and, consequently, its ventilation, heating, and energy requirements. This article proposes a trans-domain digital twin framework with single-episode learning capability, customized for bio-aware climate and energy control in a closed cattle-fattening barn. The framework integrates a mechanistic climate simulator, a livestock growth simulator, model predictive control, lightweight reinforcement learning, and structured knowledge memory within a multi-rate temporal-loop architecture. The fast temporal loop operates every five minutes to evaluate actuator decisions and maintain short-term thermal comfort, safety, and energy efficiency, whereas the slow temporal loop provides biological guidance based on daily climatic conditions, feed efficiency, heat production, and growth-limiting factors. The results show that climate, growth, energy, feed, biological guidance, and memory can be linked within a single executable control cycle. Remaining limitations include the need for field validation, improved management of feed pressure, and reduction of abrupt actuator-command variations.

</details>


### [24] [Twelve Quick Tips for Managing IT Disasters in Small Research Software Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27196)
*Greg Wilson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 为非系统管理员的单人或小团队提供简单可行的灾难规划与恢复指南，说明何为达成目标，并鼓励利用机构支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强调研究计算系统容易受到各种突发事件（如服务中断、自然灾害等）影响，提示小型团队提前规划以降低风险与缩短恢复时间。

Method: 采用实用导向的方法，面向非专业系统管理员，提供具体的步骤、清单与建议，并建议与机构内的研究计算组、数据馆员与安全与环境办公室协作以获得专业支持。

Result: 给出一组可操作的灾难应对与恢复措施、‘完成’的判定标准，以及在资源受限情况下如何利用机构资源达成目标。

Conclusion: 本论文结论是通过规范化灾难规划流程与明确“完成”标准，小型研究软件团队能够在资源有限的情况下显著提高研究计算的弹性与可恢复性。

Abstract: In 2025, the US government launched an unprecedented series of attacks on its own scientific research groups. A year later GitHub dropped below 90% availability for the first time, while wildfires in Canada, France, Spain, and elsewhere forced researchers from the homes and labs. These events and others have reminded us just how fragile research computing systems can be, and that planning for disasters is one of the most effective ways to prevent them.
  This paper is a short guide to disaster planning and recovery for a small research software team. The tips assume you are doing everything yourself on top of your regular job, and that you aren't an experienced system administrator. Some of the tips do require that kind of expertise, but most research institutions have research computing groups, data librarians, and environmental health-and-safety offices whose entire job is to help with exactly these problems. This paper tells you what "done" looks like; they can often provide it.

</details>


### [25] [Persona-Execution Separation: An Architecture Pattern for Evolving LLM Agents under Execution Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27427)
*Yisen Xi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PES：把persona与执行分离到不同信任域，用受治理合约桥、DLP和审计保证信息流，满足persona自由漂移与执行可审计性的折中，实际实现需谨慎确保工件不可逆回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受治理组织中既要让LLM agent的人设灵活演化，又要保证有状态执行被审计和可追溯，单一信任域难以在成本可接受范围内同时满足两者。

Method: 定义设计目标（自由漂移、执行可追溯、解耦），提出架构（单宿主persona、面无人格execution、受控合约桥、DLP、审批矩阵、审计锚点）；形式论证在LLM表示不可辨识条件下的不可替代性；以受监管数字员工平台的开发/试点为案例执行实现与机制检查、回溯构建和探针测试。

Result: 提出PES架构并给出论证；在真实平台上记录五个决策与被拒替代项；发现产品实现缺少关键执行侧再验证与persona指纹，回溯构建显示隔离是通过省略连接实现而非架构强制，表明潜在回退风险。

Conclusion: 提出Persona-Execution Separation (PES)作为架构模式，把persona和execution放在不同信任域，通过受治理的合约桥连接，实现persona自由漂移与执行可追溯性；在LLM表示不可辨识假设下，任何单域机制要达成相同目标需重构为类似PES但耦合更高。实际开发案例证明实现存在缺陷，强调PES作为审计规则的必要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents in governed organizations must let the persona (instructions, tone, self-presentation) evolve freely, while keeping execution (stateful, audited work) traceable. A single trust domain does not satisfy both cheaply. We present Persona-Execution Separation (PES): persona and execution reside in different trust domains, connected by a governed contract bridge. The persona is singly-homed and may drift; execution is faceless and audited. Status summaries may return; data bodies remain in the restrictive domain except a graded data-loss-prevention (DLP) exception; identity stays continuous. An approval matrix, DLP, and audit enforce the crossing. PES follows from three goals---free drift, execution traceability, and decoupling. Under LLM representational indistinguishability, any single-domain mechanism that meets all three must re-introduce typed change objects, an external gate, and a stable audit anchor: PES rebuilt at higher coupling cost. A development/pilot case in a regulated digital-employee platform records five decisions over one month, each with a rejected alternative. A mechanism check on the shipped implementation found no execution-side re-validation under persona perturbation (five model configurations) and no persona fingerprint on hard-asserted fields. A probe of a recovered pre-separation build found the governed execution path decoupled from the persona by omission, not by construction; a later wiring change could reverse that isolation, which PES makes an audited architectural rule. The pattern applies when multi-user deployment, execution audit, and expected persona churn hold jointly.

</details>


### [26] [From Static to Dynamic: Benchmarking Real-World Code Review with MCR-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27442)
*Dewu Zheng,Yanlin Wang,Xiwen Wang,Kefeng Duan,Hongyu Zhang,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MCR-Bench提供真实多轮代码评审的缺陷状态标注与基准测试，实验证明当前主流LLM在多轮、跨轮状态跟踪与复杂缺陷检测方面存在明显短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化代码评审研究多将评审简化为单轮静态决策，不能反映现实中多轮交互与缺陷演化过程，因而需要一个能刻画缺陷状态随多轮演进的基准来更真实地评估模型能力。

Method: 作者构建并公开一个包含2,269个真实多轮评审任务、覆盖5种编程语言的数据集。每个任务带有缺陷描述、类型、严重性等元信息，并对跨轮缺陷状态做动态注解。基于该基准，对主流LLM进行大规模实验，并通过错误分析剖析误判原因（如跨轮时间错位、长程记忆不足）。

Result: 发布了MCR-Bench数据集（2,269任务，5语言），并得到三类主要发现：1）整体能力受限，且随轮次增加下降明显；2）性能受缺陷类型与严重度影响显著，语义复杂或低显著性缺陷易被忽视；3）错误分析揭示关键失败机制，如跨轮时间对齐问题与长程记忆不足。

Conclusion: 本文提出了MCR-Bench——首个面向真实多轮代码评审的缺陷状态感知基准，包含细粒度缺陷元数据与跨轮状态标注；在基准上评测主流大语言模型，发现其在缺陷检测与生命周期追踪上能力有限，且随交互轮数增加性能显著下降。

Abstract: In real-world software development, code review typically involves iterative interactions between developers and reviewers to improve software quality, making the process costly and time-consuming. Although recent work explores large language models (LLMs) for automated code review, most approaches oversimplify code review into a single-round, static decision task, which fails to capture the multi-round interactive nature and the complex problem-solving processes inherent in realistic review scenarios. To bridge this gap, we introduce MCR-Bench, the first defect state-aware benchmark designed for realistic multi-round code review. MCR-Bench covers five commonly-used programming languages and consists of 2,269 real-world multi-round code review tasks, each of which is annotated with fine-grained defect information and cross-round state labels. Each task in MCR-Bench is equipped with fine-grained defect metadata (e.g., description, type, severity) alongside dynamic state annotations, capturing the complete evolutionary trajectory of a defect throughout the multi-round process. We obtain several findings through extensive experiments on MCR-Bench with mainstream LLMs. (1) Limited overall capability: experiments reveal that mainstream LLMs exhibit limited overall performance in defect detection and defect lifecycle state tracking, with performance degrading significantly as the number of interaction rounds increases; (2) Defect-sensitive performance: LLMs' performance varies substantially across different defect types and severity levels, with semantically complex or low-salience defects being significantly more likely to be missed; (3) Underlying Failure Mechanisms: our in-depth error analysis dissects the distinct drivers of false positives and false negatives, revealing critical weaknesses such as cross-round temporal misalignment and inadequate long-range memory.

</details>


### [27] [SWE-Prime: Fewer Trajectories, Better Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27449)
*Dewu Zheng,Ruizhe Ye,Yanlin Wang,Yang Ye,Hongyu Zhang,Ensheng Shi,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Jianxing Yu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Prime通过轨迹级和片段级的两阶段筛选，去噪并保留有用步骤，使少量高质量数据比全量成功轨迹更能提升模型解决软件问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用‘成功’轨迹作监督存在噪声——成功并不等于高质量，轨迹中可能包含低效、冗余或有风险的步骤，促使模型学习不良问题解决行为，需对训练数据进行精细筛选以提升学习效率与行为质量。

Method: 提出两阶段的多粒度SFT数据选择方法：第一阶段在轨迹级基于过程质量、结果质量和数据代表性筛选出高质量成功轨迹子集；第二阶段将连续步骤分成语义片段，评估每个片段对最终解的贡献、可学习性和潜在风险。在SFT时保留所有片段以维护上下文，但仅对被选择的片段计算损失。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Pro与SWE-Bench Verified上，使用SWE-Prime选出的10%轨迹子集训练，分别在基准上较用全部已解决数据训练获得最高达12.2%与24.2%的相对性能提升，证实了数据选择的有效性与样本效率提升。

Conclusion: SWE-Prime通过对成功轨迹在轨迹级和片段级进行逐步筛选，去除低质量、冗余或带风险的步骤，从而为监督微调提供更精准的训练信号，显著提升了解决软件工程任务的大型语言模型性能。

Abstract: To improve large language models' ability to resolve real-world software issues, prior work has focused on constructing large-scale agent trajectory datasets and performing supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on successful trajectories. However, task success does not guarantee high-quality supervision: successful trajectories may still contain ineffective, redundant, or risky steps. Directly using such trajectories for SFT can introduce noisy supervision and encourage models to imitate undesirable problem-solving behaviors. Therefore, we propose SWE-Prime, a multi-granularity, two-stage SFT data selection method that progressively filters training data at the trajectory and segment levels. Specifically, the first stage performs trajectory-level screening based on process quality, result quality, and data representativeness, selecting a high-quality and representative subset of successful trajectories. The second stage performs segment-level selection by grouping consecutive steps into semantic segments and assessing each segment based on its contribution to the final solution, learnability, and potential risks. During SFT, all segments remain in the sequence to preserve context, while only selected segments contribute to the loss computation. Experiments on SWE-Bench Pro and SWE-Bench Verified show that training on the 10% trajectory subset selected by SWE-Prime outperforms training on the full resolved dataset, yielding relative performance gains of up to 12.2% and 24.2%, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [28] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 神经-符号混合的 EduRiskX 在 OULAD 上实现了更早且更具可解释性的学业风险预测，但需要更多关于规则构建、融合训练与泛化性的细节来评估可复现性与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有风险预测模型早期检测能力不足与“黑盒”可解释性差的问题，以便在真实教学情境中获得更高的信任与可操作性。

Method: 使用带时间注意力的 Transformer 对学生纵向行为序列建模，采用类别加权损失与动态周截断；从训练数据基于教育理论自动构建 F-Logic 规则库以计算符号置信度；通过逻辑回归融合神经概率与符号置信度。

Result: 在 OULAD（学生级严格 80/10/10 划分）上，终点（第38周）准确率 0.900、F1 0.894，平均提前检测周数 9.32，检测率 94.30%，在召回率和检测提前性上优于 PatchTST、iTransformer、LSTM、CNN 等基线。

Conclusion: EduRiskX 提供了一种将时序 Transformer 与 F-Logic 符号推理相结合的神经-符号框架，用于早期预测在线教育中的学业风险，兼顾了预测性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [29] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentic multi-step LLM pipelines improved guideline grounding and some clinical detail versus standalone LLMs but reduced raw alignment with feature-attribution; combine agentic decomposition with attribution-based checks for safer high-stakes explanations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide bedside-ready clinical narratives. LLMs could translate model attributions into explanations; agentic pipelines might improve safety by decomposing interpretation, guideline checking, and explanation generation.

Method: Retrospective feasibility study using the local eICU Demo artifact set (n=2,353 ICU stays, 8.1% mortality). Predictive model: XGBoost yielding AUROC 0.855 (95% CI 0.796–0.906) and AUPRC 0.332 (95% CI 0.217–0.494). Explanations: standalone LLM versus a four-step agentic pipeline. Evaluation: stratified 38-case explanation subset (leakage and qualitative errors), and a 14-case overlap with a SHAP review subset for quantitative alignment metrics (Jaccard SHAP overlap, direction consistency) and human-rated measures (guideline grounding, value specificity, plausibility).

Result: In the full cohort, XGBoost performance AUROC 0.855 and AUPRC 0.332. In the 38-case explanation subset, standalone LLM produced one explanation with explicit outcome leakage, agentic pipeline produced none. In 14-case SHAP overlap: standalone LLM had higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9% vs 78.6%); agentic pipeline had higher guideline grounding (0.762 vs 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 vs 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 vs 0.671).

Conclusion: Agentic (multi-step) decomposition improved guideline grounding and some patient-specific detail compared to a standalone LLM explanation, while attribution-based checks (e.g., SHAP) still favored the standalone LLM for SHAP alignment and directional consistency. Overall recommendation: agentic pipelines can increase safety-relevant grounding but should be paired with attribution-based checks before clinical use.

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [30] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 综述论文主张用基于Transformer的大型模型革新电池健康管理，系统分类现有工作并提出数据生态、验证、可信性与部署四大研究方向的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法在计算效率、参数标定、跨域泛化、对标注数据的依赖以及模型可解释性方面存在瓶颈，LMs凭借大规模预训练与自监督学习可能突破这些限制，促成更安全、可靠与自治的电池管理系统。

Method: 系统性综述方法：先梳理促进LM发展的基础技术（Transformer、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据、参数高效微调等），再将现有工作按四个维度分类讨论（数据稀缺、泛化与鲁棒、领域知识融合以提升可解释性、系统级自动化），并提出面向未来的研究路线图与建议。

Result: 给出LM在BPHM应用的系统分类与分析、列出现有进展与不足、提出围绕协作数据生态、工业化智能验证、物理信息增强可信性与高效边缘部署的路线图，为研究者与工程实践者提供发展指引。

Conclusion: 本文综述认为基于Transformer的大型模型（LMs）为电池健康管理（BPHM）提供了新范式，能在缓解数据稀缺、提升跨域泛化、增强可解释性与实现系统自动化方面带来显著潜力，但在数据可及性、智能性验证、可信性与部署可行性等方面仍有重要挑战需解决。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [31] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PICasso：从自然语言到GDS的光子芯片自动合成-验证-优化框架，结合PDK知识、自动布局布线、DRC/LVS和SAX仿真，并构建36题的PIC-Set基准，对多款LLM进行统一评测，展示在规格满足率和插入损耗优化上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低光子集成电路设计门槛，使自然语言需求能自动转化为可制造布局，并系统评估AI在光子设计中可行性与局限。

Method: 设计NL->YAML->GDS流水线，注入PDK相关知识，自动放布线、DRC/LVS校验，基于SAX的光子仿真，并用仿真引导的优化循环改进性能。用PIC-Set对若干SOTA LLM在统一协议下评测，提出结构/功能Spec@k等新指标。

Result: 在PIC-Set上，PICasso比原始LLM生成显著提高端到端规范满足率；结构Spec@3最高达92.7%，功能Spec@3高达52%；平均插入损耗从4.98 dB降至3.25 dB（改进1.74 dB）。

Conclusion: 结构化域约束+物理验证+仿真反馈能将LLM从脆弱的网表生成器转变为能产出可制造布局的实用PIC设计代理，在规范满足率、损耗优化和运行时上均有竞争力。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation.
